### [1] [On the Geometric Structure of Hyperbolic Clifford Bundles and Associated Spin Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03886)
*Eduardo Notte-Cuello*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了实n维向量空间V上双曲Clifford代数Cl(H_V)的旋量结构，定义了相应的Clifford-Lipschitz群、Pin群与Spin群，并建立了其标架丛与旋量结构；该代数统一容纳多重形式与多重向量，被称为‘母代数’，适用于超场理论与引力论中的双曲结构推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在理论物理（特别是超场理论与引力理论）中统一处理多重形式与多重向量，并将Clifford丛形式推广至双曲几何结构，需构造适配双曲度量的Clifford代数及其旋量表示框架。

Method: 基于实n维向量空间V上的双曲二次型H_V，构造双曲Clifford代数Cl(H_V)；定义其正交群、Clifford-Lipschitz群、Pin群与Spin群；通过主丛与提升技术导出对应于Cl(H_V)的标架丛及旋量结构。

Result: 确立了Cl(H_V)的完备代数结构及其对称性群（O(H_V), Pin(H_V), Spin(H_V)）；首次系统构建了双曲Clifford代数上的旋量结构与标架丛，证明其可作为超场与双曲引力理论的自然代数基础。

Conclusion: 双曲Clifford代数Cl(H_V)不仅统一了多向量与多形式，而且其旋量结构为超对称场论与双曲时空引力模型（如AdS相关背景）提供了严格的微分几何与代数框架，是标准Clifford分析向不定号度量情形的实质性推广。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the spinor structure associated with the Hyperbolic Clifford algebra of a real $n$-dimensional vector space $V$, which is denoted by $\mathcal{C}l\left(H_{V}\right)$. Unlike the standard Clifford algebra, the Hyperbolic Clifford algebra $\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ simultaneously accommodates both multiforms and multivectors in a single algebraic structure, making it the natural framework---known as the ``mother algebra''---for the study of superfields in theoretical physics and for generalizing the Clifford bundle formalism to hyperbolic structures arising in gravitational theories. The orthogonal groups and orthogonal transformations associated to the hyperbolic space $H_{V}$ are presented. The Clifford--Lipschitz group and the Pin and Spin groups associated with $\mathcal{C}l\left(H_{V}\right)$ are defined. Then, the frame bundle and spinor structure associated to Hyperbolic Clifford algebra is derived.

</details>


### [2] [A hybrid quantum-classical lattice kinetic scheme with tensor-train feedback for incompressible flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04500)
*Yongwei Guo,Yuan Yu,Yue Yu,Yuting Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量训练（TT）的量子-经典混合接口，用于非线性格子动力学模拟，通过保留可重用的经典核心、能量缩放传播与坐标恢复、交替最小二乘重构等技术，在避免重复量子演化的同时保障非线性反馈精度；理论给出扰动与资源界限，并通过数值实验揭示分布误差不足以表征物理量（如速度）精度，强调压缩秩、速度保真度与读出精度需分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性格子动力学中量子-经典耦合模拟的高读出开销问题：全分布读出导致量子资源随格点数线性增长，且传统误差度量（如分布L2误差）无法保证关键物理量（如速度场）的收敛性。

Method: 构建TT格式接口，将格子分布表示为低秩张量训练；采用物理振幅尺度下的可重用经典核心编码非线性系数并准备量子输入；利用能量缩放传播与坐标恢复隐式编码更新分布；通过符号化Hadamard测试获取重叠，再以交替最小二乘（ALS）结合已知归一化因子重构输出TT核心；反馈循环直接从接受的核心启动，无需回溯量子演化；理论分析基于正则参考系数、密度上下界及Lipschitz平衡敏感性建立局部缺陷与反馈误差的扰动界。

Result: 在16×16网格80步反馈中，TT秩上限为4时虽分布误差<1%，但涡旋结构几乎消失；秩上限为12时相对速度误差降至1.03×10⁻³；四步二项读出实验表明，采样容差收紧三倍使所需总测量次数从5.97×10¹²增至5.37×10¹³，趋近固定秩/固定扫掠的理想轨迹；资源界涵盖经典运算、电路执行、采样与验证开销。

Conclusion: 该TT接口实现了量子传播与非线性反馈的高效、可控耦合；证明分布保真度不等价于流场物理保真度；确立了压缩秩选择、速度精度目标与读出统计精度三者间的定量权衡关系，为实用化量子增强流体模拟提供了可验证的架构基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear lattice kinetic simulation requires each updated distribution to supply the velocity and gradients that determine the next step. In a quantum-classical solver, reading out the full distribution incurs work proportional to the lattice size. We construct a tensor-train (TT) interface that retains reusable classical cores at the physical amplitude scale for a full-relaxation lattice kinetic scheme. The cores supply nonlinear coefficients and prepare each quantum input. Energy-scaled propagation and coordinate recovery encode the updated distribution, and alternating least-squares reconstruction converts signed Hadamard-test overlaps into output cores by restoring a known normalization factor. The next step starts from accepted cores without replaying previous quantum evolution. Under positive, unit-sum reference coefficients, positive lower and finite upper density bounds, and Lipschitz equilibrium sensitivity, perturbation bounds relate local defects to feedback errors. Resource bounds account for classical operations, circuit execution, sampling, and verification. Classical diagnostics expose why distribution accuracy alone is insufficient: in 80-step feedback on a $16\times16$ grid, rank cap 4 nearly eliminates the vortex despite a final distribution error below $1\%$, whereas rank cap 12 gives relative velocity error $1.03\times10^{-3}$. Four-step binomial readout simulations show that tightening core sampling tolerance threefold raises the prescribed sufficient budget from $5.97\times10^{12}$ to $5.37\times10^{13}$ shots as the result approaches the fixed-rank, fixed-sweep exact-overlap trajectory. The interface therefore provides a way to connect quantum propagation to nonlinear feedback and to assess the separate demands of compression, velocity accuracy, and readout precision.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文定义并研究了O*-代数上关于某元素A的本征态，并将其应用于满足特定对易关系的阶梯算子族，探讨了此类态的若干性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期相关结果启发，旨在推广量子力学中本征态的概念至更一般的O*-代数框架，并为具有物理意义的代数结构（如阶梯算子）提供严格数学刻画。

Method: 基于O*-代数理论，引入‘关于元素A的本征态’这一新概念，结合代数泛函分析与算子理论方法，推导其基本性质；随后将该定义应用于满足物理动机的阶梯型对易关系的元素族。

Result: 得到了本征态的存在性、谱特性及与阶梯算子代数结构相容的一系列性质，包括正交性、归一化条件及在特定表示下的实现形式。

Conclusion: 所定义的本征态为非正规算子或无界算子的谱理论提供了新工具，且在量子场论与可积系统等物理语境中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Following some recent results, we consider here a particular class of states on a $O^\ast$-algebra $\M$ which we call {\em eigenstates of an element} $A\in\M$, and we deduce some of their properties. Then, we apply our definition to a family of ladder elements, i.e. elements of $\M$ obeying certain algebraic (commutation) relations which are physically motivated, and we discuss several results for these states

</details>


### [4] [An Excursion into Grassmannian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04613)
*Dmitri Bykov,Viacheslav Krivorol*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了复Grassmann流形上Laplace-Beltrami与Laplace-Bochner算子的经典谱问题，提出两种源于一维sigma模型量子化的互补方法：一是基于SU(n)-等变GLSM的一阶构造，给出以投影算子和Plücker坐标表示的显式本征函数；二是利用自旋链实现，通过长矩形SU(n)表示的极限张量积刻画L²函数空间（含磁单极情形），从而统一阐释调和分析、杨图、磁丛与拉普拉斯谱之间的几何关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非交换调和分析方法虽系统但缺乏显式构造；亟需发展能直接关联几何结构（如Plücker嵌入、磁丛）与谱数据（本征值/本征函数）的物理启发新方法。

Method: 1) SU(n)-等变第一类广义线性西格玛模型（GLSM）构造，导出以投影算子与Plücker坐标表达的Laplace-Bochner本征函数；2) 自旋链类比，将L²空间（或磁性截面空间）实现为长矩形SU(n)最高权表示的Gelfand–Tsetlin极限张量积。

Result: 获得复Grassmann流形上Laplace-Beltrami与Laplace-Bochner算子的显式本征函数族；建立Young图（对应SU(n)不可约表示最高权）、磁U(1)丛拓扑荷、Plücker几何坐标与本征值谱之间的精确字典；证明两种方法在特征标级与算子级均一致。

Conclusion: 复Grassmann流形的谱几何可由sigma模型量子化框架完全重构：GLSM提供微分几何层面的显式解，自旋链方法则揭示其表示论本质；二者共同确立了调和分析、代数几何（Plücker嵌入）与拓扑（磁荷）的统一描述。

Abstract: We revisit the classical spectral problem for the Laplace-Beltrami and Laplace-Bochner operators on complex Grassmannians. After reviewing the standard approach based on non-commutative harmonic analysis, we develop two complementary methods originating from the quantization of the corresponding one-dimensional sigma model, both with and without a magnetic monopole field. The first is based on $\mathbf{SU}(n)$-equivariant first-order GLSM formulation and gives explicit eigenfunctions in terms of projectors and Plücker coordinates. The second uses a spin-chain realization of free particles on manifolds and describes the space of square-integrable functions, or sections in the magnetic case, through limiting tensor products of long rectangular $\mathbf{SU}(n)$ representations. Together, these approaches provide a more explicit picture of the spectral geometry of Grassmannians and clarify the relation between harmonics, Young diagrams, magnetic bundles, and Laplacian spectra.

</details>

<details>

Abstract: We construct a measure on continuous paths in the future cone of two-dimensional space that is invariant under the orthochronous Lorentz group and quasi-invariant under the action of the diffeomorphism group. We use the proposed measure to obtain the fundamental solution of the heat equation. The result agrees with the expression obtained by other methods.

</details>

Method: 利用自相似解的轮廓函数（profile），通过解析构造方法显式写出对应于本征值λ=5的本征函数，揭示其普适性根源。

Result: 证明了该最小不稳定本征值λ=5所对应的本征函数可由自相似轮廓函数直接、初等地构造得出，从而解释了其在所有激发解中的普适性。

Conclusion: λ=5的普适性源于自相似结构本身的对称性与轮廓函数的微分关系，并非偶然；该结果为Yang-Mills奇点动力学提供了关键谱分析基础。

Abstract: Self-similar solutions play an important role in the formation of singularities in the Yang--Mills equations. In five space dimensions, these equations admit infinitely many self-similar solutions with an increasing number of unstable modes. It was observed numerically that all excited profiles share the least unstable eigenvalue $\lambda=5$. We explain this remarkable spectral property by an elementary construction of the corresponding eigenfunction in terms of the profile.

</details>


### [7] [Multiplicity and parity in Hirota's $N$-soliton conditions: the bilinear families $L\,(D_x^{3}+Λ)^{2n+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04953)
*Shi Zhenhua*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Hirota三孤子条件与N孤子可解性之间的逻辑蕴含关系，聚焦于两类广义双线性方程族：一类是形如L(D_x^3+Λ)^{2n+1}f·f=0的奇次幂算子族（含KdV、Ito、BLMP等经典方程），另一类是具有图结构、二次曲面、抛物型及KP型色散关系的更一般双线性方程；通过代数几何方法（如双交替量、符号和、平方和证书）严格证明：对n≥1，三孤子条件恒成立但四孤子条件普遍失效（验证Gürses–Pekcan猜想），并给出非退化孤子数的精确上界（如n=1时为4或5个），同时在多种色散类型下完成N-孤子可解性的完全分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清‘三孤子条件蕴含所有N孤子解’这一 folklore 命题的适用范围与失效边界，修正并严格化Hietarinta分类纲领的基础假设，为高阶可积系统提供代数可积性判据。

Method: 运用Hirota双线性方法、代数几何中的色散簇（dispersion variety）分解、主分支（principal component）上的双交替量（bialternant）分析、符号求和计数、四孤子残差（four-soliton residual）显式构造、平方和（SOS）证书判定实解存在性，以及对图型、二次型、抛物型和KP型色散关系的系统分类。

Result: 1) 对L(D_x^3+Λ)^{2n+1}f·f=0族：n=0时Hirota可积；n≥1时三孤子条件恒成立，但四孤子残差非零，故四孤子解不存在；给出显式SOS证书排除所有实参数配置下的四孤子解；确定最大非退化孤子数：n=1时为4或5（取决于L是否属于span{D_x,Λ}），n=2且L=D_x时为5，任意n≥1均有有限上界。2) 对单重非超平面色散分支，完成图型L(q(D)+D_t)、不可约偶次二次型、抛物型、F(ℓ)+Q含KP曲面型、及带指定单参相位族的三次图型等情形的N-孤子可解性完全分类。

Conclusion: ‘三孤子条件⇒N-孤子可解’并非普适真理；其有效性高度依赖于色散关系的代数结构；本文建立了首个针对高阶奇次算子族的严格反例体系，并提供了多种几何类型下可积性的精细分类框架，将Hirota方法提升至代数可积性理论层面。

Abstract: Hirota's three-soliton condition is the standard test for the integrability of a bilinear equation $P(D)f\cdot f=0$, and the folklore implication ``three-soliton condition $\Rightarrow$ $N$-soliton solutions for all $N$'' underlies the classifications of Hietarinta. We study this implication in two directions. The first is the family $L\,(D_x^3+\Lambda)^{2n+1}f\cdot f=0$ with $L$, $\Lambda$ linear forms in the Hirota operators, which for $n=0$ contains the Korteweg--de Vries, Ito and Boiti--Leon--Manna--Pempinelli equations and for $L=D_x$ is the family of Gürses and Pekcan. On the principal component of the dispersion variety the $N$-soliton condition reduces to a sign sum of two bialternants; counting odd exponents shows that for $n=0$ the class is Hirota integrable and that for $n\ge1$ the three-soliton condition holds for every $L$, and an explicit formula for the four-soliton residual proves the Gürses--Pekcan conjecture that the four-soliton condition fails for every $n\ge1$. The formula yields sum-of-squares certificates excluding four-soliton solutions at every real configuration for the Gürses--Pekcan and Ito equations, classifies the real members of the class, and determines maximal numbers of nondegenerate solitons: exactly four or five for $n=1$ according as $L$ lies in the span of $D_x,\Lambda$ or not, five for $n=2$ and $L=D_x$, and finite for every $n\ge1$ and every $L$. The second direction is a reformulation of the implication for a single non-hyperplane component of multiplicity one, which we prove, with complete classifications, for all graph-type dispersions $L(q(D)+D_t)$, for every irreducible even quadric component in any dimension and with an arbitrary cofactor, for parabolic components, for the dispersions $F(\ell)+Q$ containing the Kadomtsev--Petviashvili surface, and, with a prescribed one-parameter phase family, for the cubic graph.

</details>


### [8] [High-order compact ALE gas-kinetic scheme on three-dimensional unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05095)
*Yibo Wang,Xing Ji,Liang Pan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向三维非结构网格的紧凑型高阶气体动理学格式（HGKS），用于任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）可压缩流计算；通过内存优化的二次多项式重构、GENO限制器与梯度压缩因子抑制振荡，并采用多GPU（CUDA+MPI）加速实现高效显式求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升ALE框架下高阶格式在三维非结构网格上的计算效率，克服传统高阶重构中频繁矩阵求逆与每步几何更新导致的开销瓶颈。

Method: 基于平均梯度的最小二乘法构建紧致二次多项式重构（仅需存储3×3系数矩阵、四次LS求解）；引入GENO重构与梯度压缩因子控制激波附近振荡；开发基于单元级并行的多GPU CUDA+MPI混合加速框架。

Result: 方案在强间断流、俯仰翼型及直升机旋翼等算例中展现出高精度（高阶收敛性）、强鲁棒性与优异并行可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该紧凑HGKS-ALE格式在保持高阶精度与非结构适应性的同时，显著降低了内存占用与重构成本，为复杂几何下高保真可压缩流动模拟提供了高效可行的数值工具。

Abstract: In this paper, a compact high-order gas-kinetic scheme (HGKS) is developed for compressible arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) computations on three-dimensional unstructured meshes. To achieve high-order spatial accuracy, a large number of matrix inversions are required for spatial reconstruction. In ALE computations, the mesh moves and the geometric information must be recalculated at each time step, which reduces computational efficiency. To improve efficiency, a memory-reduction reconstruction strategy is employed to construct a quadratic polynomial on unstructured meshes. By differentiating the candidate polynomial, the quadratic terms are obtained by the least-squares method using the average gradient values of the cell itself and its neighboring cells. The linear terms of the polynomial are also determined by the least-squares method. On three-dimensional unstructured meshes, a $3\times3$ coefficient matrix must be stored, and four least-squares procedures are required to construct a quadratic polynomial. To suppress numerical oscillations in flows with discontinuities, generalized essentially non-oscillatory (GENO) reconstruction and a gradient compression factor are adopted. To accelerate the computation, a multi-GPU accelerated code is implemented using Compute Unified Device Architecture (CUDA) and Message Passing Interface (MPI). The current scheme is fully explicit, and the acceleration framework can be developed based on cell-level parallelism. Numerical examples are presented to evaluate the accuracy and robustness of the scheme, including flows with strong discontinuities, pitching wings, and helicopter rotors. Numerical results confirm the excellent performance of the compact ALE scheme on unstructured meshes.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method based on rasterization in computer graphics for the integration of discontinuous functions accurately and efficiently on the GPU. The proposed method takes advantage of the massively parallel architecture of GPUs via a modified graphics pipeline, where discontinuities are encoded through geometric primitives and processed in the rasterization stage, and multisample anti-aliasing (MSAA) is used to improve integration accuracy across subdomain interfaces. We present both hardware- and software-based pipelines and compare their performance in terms of accuracy as well as computational effort on modern GPU hardware with adaptive quadrature on the CPU. For the tested hardware, at approximately comparable median relative error, the hardware pipeline is up to around $100\text{x}$ faster than adaptive quadrature, while the single- and double-precision software pipelines achieve up to $840\text{x}$ and $1200\text{x}$ speedups compared to their adaptive quadrature counterparts. We use the finite cell method as model problem, where cutcells represent discontinuous integrands at the interface of the physical and fictitious domains and demonstrate the performance of the proposed method using numerical benchmarks. The end-to-end finite cell solution runtime is reduced by a factor of $8.3$ to $9.5$ in the single-precision pipeline and by a factor of $62$ to $72$ in the double-precision pipeline at the prescribed integration settings. Therefore, results indicate that the rasterization-based integration method is a promising alternative for the accurate and efficient integration of discontinuous functions in demanding applications on the GPU.

It is known that $D_{A,c}^\epsilon \to D_{A,c}$ as $\epsilon\to 0+$ and that $D_{A,c}-c^2/2$ converges, as $c\to+\infty$, to the Schrödinger operator $H_A$ with the general point interaction, both in the norm resolvent sense. We prove that the first convergence is uniform with respect to $c$ in a neighbourhood of $+\infty$, and we provide explicit rates of convergence. Combining this with an explicit computation of the non-relativistic limit of $D_{A,c}^\epsilon$ for fixed $\epsilon$, we show that the two limits can be interchanged and that the corresponding double limit exists. As a by-product we obtain a family of non-local, rank-two approximations $H_A^\epsilon$ of $H_A$ that converge to $H_A$ in the norm resolvent sense. Contrary to the relativistic setting, the coupling constants of $H_A^\epsilon$ are renormalized; we give an explicit formula for this renormalization.

Abstract: SecRed is a C++ program for the reduction of multi-loop Feynman integrals with integration-by-parts (IBP) identities. It reduces an integral family sector by sector for memory control and parallelism, and adds algorithms designed for that setting: an adaptive seed box with a corner cut, an optional seed truncation, a subsector level filter, and symmetry relations expanded only from the master integrals of a sector. A global stage assembles the retained equations and performs the finite-field reconstruction of the coefficients using FiniteFlow or FireFly, repeating the elimination over a sequence of primes. On the elimination side, a coefficient-free plan records the elimination order once and is replayed at each prime, so that only the arithmetic is repeated, and a head score orders the equations to reduce fill-in during the elimination. In this paper, we address this problem by introducing a semiparametric estimation framework. The key advantage of our approach is that it avoids the need to refit the PDFs in the presence of either modified SM parameters or BSM contributions. Starting from standard, decoupled estimates of the PDFs and physics parameters, the method computes a bias correction a posteriori via the efficient influence function, yielding a debiased one-step estimator of the finite-dimensional parameter along with uncertainties with valid frequentist coverage. This correction rigorously accounts for the interplay between PDF and physics parameters without requiring a computationally expensive joint fit. Abstract: We study back-to-back dijet production in deep inelastic scattering (DIS) at next-to-eikonal accuracy within the Color Glass Condensate framework, focusing on the quark-background contributions. Our analysis extends the operator-level connection between the next-to-eikonal CGC framework and quark TMDs beyond leading power in the correlation limit.

Currently, LHCb has a sample of charmed meson decays with around 100 million events for the $D^+ \to K^-K^+\pi^+$ decay, so a more robust phenomenological model is needed to describe such a large data set. The present model has allowed the inclusion of hadronic FSI and will, in prospect, introduce three-body effects, overcoming some limitations of the Isobar Model, and has all factors necessary to reveal the presence of CPV. To do so, it factorizes the weak vertex separated in leading topologies and relies on extensions of Resonant Chiral Perturbation Theory to describe FSI. From fits to model-independent parametrizations of scattering amplitudes, the parameters of the FSI amplitudes were fixed, and the production parameters of the weak vertex were then obtained from the fit to the toy dataset, yielding a good agreement between the data and our model.

On this basis, we derive an expression for the fully generic chiral anomaly directly from the contributions of the bubble and triangle diagrams, either in the massless or massive case. Then, we show that the different forms of the anomaly can arise from appropriate choices of parameters directly at the level of this master expression. Overall, $d=2$ is shown to provide an excellent environment to investigate chiral anomalies at the amplitude level: Ward identities are demonstrated rigorously and to arbitrary order in the mass expansion without further approximation, resulting in an unprecedented degree of control on the anomaly.

Abstract: A search for a Majorana-type heavy neutral lepton (HNL) produced in top quark decays is reported. The search is based on proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, collected during 2016$-$2018 with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC. In the type-I seesaw model, a Majorana HNL can mediate lepton-number-violating top quark decays. The search targets top quark pair production events in which one top quark decays into a bottom quark and a W boson, which subsequently decays into a charged lepton (electron or muon) and an HNL. The Majorana HNL then decays into a W boson and a charged lepton with the same sign and flavor as the one produced with the HNL. The experimental signature features a same-sign, same-flavor lepton pair and jets. The HNL masses between 20 and 100 GeV are probed. The data agree with the standard model background prediction. Observed (expected) upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the branching fraction of the top quark decay into an HNL, a charged lepton, and a bottom quark, varying between [1.1 (0.71)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ and [4.8 (3.0)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ in the dielectron channel, and between [3.7 (3.4)] $\times$10$^{-6}$ and [2.4 (1.3)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ in the dimuon channel.

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) are a common benchmark for experiments at the lifetime frontier, as their suppressed couplings to Standard Model particles can render them long-lived on detector scales. This article presents the projected sensitivity of the ANUBIS detector at the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) to two scenarios: a photophobic ALP model and a modified version of the flavour-diagonal, fermion-coupled ALP benchmark of the Physics Beyond Colliders initiative that introduces an additional $h\to Za$ decay. For the photophobic case, the ANUBIS detector will reach a peak sensitivity of $\sigma_{Wa}\cdot Br(a\rightarrow gg)=6.2\times10^{-4}$~pb at $c\tau=1.95$ m for an ALP mass of $10$ GeV. This will improve on the current exclusion limits from the ATLAS experiment by two orders of magnitude and extend the $c\tau$ coverage by three orders of magnitude. For the fermion-coupled case with a dimension-seven Higgs--ALP interaction, the peak sensitivity to the fermion coupling is $C_{a^\Phi}=4.4\times10^{-8}$, reached for ALP masses between $16$ and $23$ GeV. Overall, the ANUBIS detector is shown to provide unique coverage of the parameter space of both models, in regions that can be probed neither by the main LHC experiments nor by forward detectors at the HL-LHC.

Abstract: Bit threads express holographic entropy as the maximal flux of a divergenceless vector field subject to a local capacity bound, but do not identify a physical carrier of that flux. In our previous work, we identified the projected string charge density with a bit-thread flow, reproduced the entropies of BTZ and ten-dimensional three-charge black holes, and showed that the maximal entropy flux is preserved under S- and T-dualities. However, the norm bound was imported from the bit-thread prescription, while the string-current configurations were prescribed along known bit-thread lines. In this paper, we give a stringy foundation for the local capacity and construct dynamical probe-string representatives of maximal flow. At genus zero and two-derivative order, the conical variation of the target-space action derived from the off-shell sphere fixes the string-frame entropy density. The gravitational replica construction and weighted flow-cut duality then yield the string-frame norm bound. We next find regular stationary F1 solutions in an NS-NS supported BTZ black hole whose spatial parts are logarithmic spirals. A uniform ensemble produces a conserved, constant-norm projected string charge density and realizes a maximal flow whose flux equals the BTZ entropy. We finally extend the construction to BTZ backgrounds with mixed NS-NS and RR flux. Varying the flux fraction changes the stationary F1 configurations at fixed metric and dilaton, while the corresponding conserved flows reproduce the same leading BTZ entropy. Thus, both the norm bound and its physical maximal-flow representative arise from string theory, with stationary strings providing physical channels for the entropic flux represented geometrically by bit threads.

Abstract: We present a calculation of the first-order metric perturbation arising from a point mass moving on a circular, equatorial orbit in Kerr spacetime. The metric perturbation is specifically aimed at meeting the requirements of second-order self-force theory: it is highly accurate; incorporates all contributions, including those associated with perturbations to the black hole's mass and angular momentum; is in Lorenz gauge near the worldline and in (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge at large radius; and can be efficiently evaluated across the entire exterior spacetime. The Lorenz gauge gives a well-understood singular structure at the particle, while the (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge ensures the asymptotic fall-off needed for an asymptotically flat second-order source. Our formalism reconstructs the metric from separable Teukolsky-type equations, decomposed into spin-weighted spheroidal harmonic modes and solved spectrally on hyperboloidal slices. The resulting $(\ell,m)$-mode sums converge rapidly across most of the domain, but slowly near the point mass; we overcome this with a novel use of a puncture to extract accurate $m$-mode data, yielding output accurate to $\sim 10^{-10}$ per $m$-mode and suitable for use at second order. Our metric-perturbation results cover a range of Kerr spins $-0.9 \le a/M \le 0.9$ and orbital radii out to $50M$. In addition to its immediate application in second-order self-force calculations, our results can also be used as input to other calculations involving Kerr metric perturbations, including studies of environmental effects and quasinormal modes.

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) and time-domain response of a massless, minimally coupled test scalar field on static, spherically symmetric covariant loop quantum black hole (cLQBH) backgrounds. Focusing on a phenomenological, Gaussian-localized holonomy profile, we obtain a localized deformation of the scalar effective potential containing both positive and negative parts. This provides a geometric realization of the bump effect, with the potential deformation and tortoise-coordinate mapping determined consistently by the same background. We characterize the deformation by its location, amplitude, and width, and study its effects using frequency-domain shooting and time-domain evolution, supplemented by matrix-pencil mode extraction and waveform reconstruction. The QNM spectrum exhibits spiral trajectories as the bump location varies, enhanced sensitivity of the overtones, and mode overtaking, whereby the branch continued from the first Schwarzschild overtone becomes longer-lived than the continued fundamental branch. Despite substantial spectral changes, the early response can remain close to its Schwarzschild counterpart, with more pronounced differences emerging later. To quantify this behavior, we introduce a local waveform-deviation time and compare it with a relative-amplitude faintness time, thereby defining an operational observation window. The deviation time depends nonmonotonically on the bump location, and suitable parameter choices allow appreciable departures to emerge before the adopted faintness threshold is reached. These results connect localized holonomy corrections with spectral sensitivity and waveform evolution, providing a basis for future gravitational-perturbation studies and assessments of gravitational-wave detectability.

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to observe gravitational-wave signals from massive black hole binaries, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and more. Despite the significant scientific potential, accurately extracting these signals from the data will only be possible if LISA's instrumental noise is understood: poor noise modelling will result in lost signals, biased parameter estimates, and incorrect astrophysical insights. Modelling LISA's instrumental noise is more challenging than its ground-based counterpart since the data is highly correlated, meaning that the noise covariance matrix can no longer be assumed diagonal in frequency and in the different data streams. In this work, we present an initial approach to perform non-parametric noise inference for LISA that requires no prior knowledge of the LISA observatory. By fitting the full noise covariance matrix with a combination of splines and inverse Lorentzians, we show that our model can estimate the power and cross spectral densities for LISA from 7 days of simulated noise-only data. We find that the median estimate from our non-parametric model whitens the data according to the expected unit normal distribution. We envision that our non-parametric model can be used as part of the global fit, as well as in combination with modelled approaches to identify, quantify, and constrain the frequency dependence of unknown noise artifacts.

</details>

Abstract: We study fully non-linear binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (EsGB) gravity with a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling function. In the absence of a well-developed initial data solver for EsGB gravity, we demonstrate that the conventional workaround solution for massless scalar fields results in confined junk radiation that contaminates late-time dynamics in the massive theory. We propose a solution to this problem by suitably modifying the equations of motion at the early stages of the simulation to tame such junk radiation, thereby allowing us to study the dynamics of the scalar field during the process of a black hole merger. This is followed by a preliminary analysis of the frequency content in the ringdown of the scalar and tensor modes. In particular, the quadrupole scalar mode exhibits a rich ringdown structure: while it is dominated by the spin-2 ``contamination'' frequency in the early stages, its late time morphology becomes primarily determined by the scalar mass frequency.

</details>

Abstract: The next generation of an international network of gravitational-wave observatories is now in the advanced planning stages. With Australia's substantial history of contributions in this field, its history of scientific mega-projects like the Square Kilometer Array, and its unique geographic location and conditions, it is ideally placed to host an observatory in the global network. It has already been demonstrated that a gravitational wave detector in Australia plays an important role in minimizing the sky localization uncertainty of gravitational wave events. We study a particular science case for an Australian observatory, focusing on its potential contributions to multimessenger astronomy. We demonstrate that an observatory in Australia can enable the detection of up to eight multimessenger gravitational-wave plus kilonova events per year, with localisations better than $1-10°^2$ in both second- and third-generation networks. We show that, in a global network of XG observatories, an Australian observatory with 2G sensitivity would still enable significant advances in multimessenger science. The full case for an Australian observatory must be considered in a much broader context; however, this work indicates it could make substantial contributions to multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [100] [Imaginary Time as a Complex Representation of Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04372)

Motivation: 澄清虚时间坐标的几何本质，避免将其简单等同于维克转动（即欧氏化），并统一处理相对论几何、作用量原理、波传播与解析延拓之间的结构性联系。

Method: 将时空嵌入复向量空间 $\mathbb{C}^{1,3}$，定义实子空间 $\operatorname{Re}(\mathbb{C}^{1,3}) \simeq \mathbb{R}^{1,3}$ 作为物理时空；构造兼容的复双线性度规 $\eta_{\mu\nu} dX^\mu dX^\nu$ 与厄米度规 $h_{\mu\nu} dX^\mu d\bar{X}^\nu$；系统重构四矢量微分几何、测地动力学、爱因斯坦方程、Klein-Gordon/Dirac 方程及其非相对论极限。

Result: 证明复表示下：(i) 所有洛伦兹协变量（如时空间隔、四动量、曲率张量）在实子空间上严格还原；(ii) 广义相对论场方程与守恒律形式不变；(iii) Kerr 与 FLRW 度规在该框架下自洽；(iv) 相对论波动方程经复时间解析延拓后自然给出薛定谔方程与欧氏路径积分结构。

Conclusion: 虚时间 $ict$ 可被赋予严格的复几何意义——作为复时空中的实子空间嵌入参数，而非仅用于计算技巧；该表示不改变物理预言，但揭示了相对论、量子力学与解析延拓之间深层的代数-几何统一性。

Abstract: We develop a systematic complex representation of Lorentzian relativity based on the imaginary temporal coordinate $X^0=ict$. Rather than treating this substitution as a Wick rotation to Euclidean spacetime, we formulate it as an invertible change of representation in which physical spacetime is identified with a real subspace of a complex vector space. This construction introduces two related metric structures: a complex-bilinear form and a Hermitian form, both of which reproduce the original Lorentzian interval when restricted to physical spacetime. We extend the representation to relativistic dynamics, including proper time, four-momentum, and the Hamilton-Jacobi formulation, and show that the metric, connection, curvature tensors, Einstein equations, and conservation laws retain their conventional content in curved spacetime. Explicit applications to the Kerr and Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker geometries demonstrate consistency for rotating and time-dependent spacetimes. We further examine relativistic wave equations and their nonrelativistic limit, showing how the Schrödinger equation and its relation to Euclidean-time evolution emerge within the same complex temporal framework. The resulting formulation introduces no new physical predictions, but clarifies the geometric role of imaginary temporal coordinates and reveals structural connections among relativistic geometry, action, wave propagation, and analytic continuation.

</details>


### [101] [Single-horizon regular black holes and the fate of the inner-horizon instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04385)
*Chen Lan,Hao Yang,Maxim Khlopov,Maxim Krasnov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了静态球对称正则黑洞的‘视界奇偶性定理’，指出正则点状中心要求度规函数f具有偶数个变号零点，从而允许单视界构型；作者构造了两类具有有限面积核心的单视界正则黑洞，并证明其满足能量条件、热力学自洽，且无内柯西视界，因而规避经典质量膨胀不稳定性；进一步，通过证明有效势非负，确立了其对标量扰动的模式稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统正则黑洞普遍含内柯西视界所引发的质量膨胀不稳定性问题，探究正则性是否必然导致双视界结构。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空的爱因斯坦方程与正则性条件（中心曲率有界、度规光滑），推导度规函数f(r)的零点分布约束，建立视界奇偶性定理；构造两类有限面积核心的单视界度规解；检验弱/主能量条件；计算表面引力与温度；构建标量扰动的Regge–Wheeler型有效势并分析其正定性。

Result: 1) 证得视界奇偶性定理：正则点状中心 ⇒ f(r)具偶数个变号零点 ⇒ 单视界（2个零点退化为1个二重零点）或双视界等构型均可能；2) 构造出外置视界与边界视界两类单视界RBH解；3) 所有解满足弱能量条件（WEC）与主能量条件（DEC）；4) 单视界构型无内柯西视界，故无经典质量膨胀；5) 标量扰动有效势V_eff ≥ 0 ⇒ l ≥ 0模式稳定。

Conclusion: 正则性本身不强制双视界结构；单视界正则黑洞在几何、物质场、热力学与线性稳定性层面均可自洽，是规避内视界不稳定性的一类物理可行模型。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) are often constructed with a regular point-like center and two horizons, including an inner Cauchy horizon susceptible to mass inflation. We show that a two-horizon structure is not a necessary consequence of regularity. For static, spherically symmetric geometries with an asymptotically static exterior, we establish a horizon-parity theorem: a regular point-like center requires an even number of sign-changing zeros of $f$. We then construct two classes of single-horizon RBHs with a finite-area core, distinguished by whether the horizon lies outside the core or coincides with it, and analyze their matter content, energy conditions, and thermodynamic properties. The absence of an inner Cauchy horizon eliminates the classical mass-inflation mechanism on the single-horizon patch. We further establish mode stability against scalar perturbations for these finite-area-core configurations by showing that the effective potential is non-negative. Our results establish how the central geometry and asymptotic structure constrain horizon multiplicity and identify single-horizon configurations that avoid the classical inner-horizon instability.

</details>


### [102] [Late-time echoes from planar wave-packet scattering by an ultracompact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04525)
*Shanshan Deng,Jian-hua He*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过时域多极模式求和法模拟了超致密无事件视界恒星中入射平面标量波的传播与散射，发现其早期响应类似黑洞环振，晚期则出现由内层稳定光圈捕获并经外势垒泄漏产生的延迟回声；多极相干叠加导致干涉效应，形成具有时序结构但峰间距交替的复杂波形；并论证第三代引力波探测器有望分辨这些微弱晚期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超致密、无事件视界的恒星（如玻色星、引力星等）在引力波探测中可能呈现的可观测特征，以区分其与黑洞的时空几何差异，特别是检验‘无毛定理’与‘黑洞互补性’等基本假设。

Method: 采用时域多极模式求和（time-domain multipole mode-sum）数值方法，对常密度超致密星体时空中的标量场波动方程进行求解，重建相干叠加后的时域波形，并与Schwarzschild黑洞结果对比；辅以信号噪声比（SNR）分析评估可探测性。

Result: 1) 早期信号呈现类黑洞环振行为，源于外部不稳定光球附近的波散射；2) 晚期出现延迟回声序列，源于内部稳定光圈处的波束缚与隧穿泄漏；3) 多极叠加引发强干涉，导致回声峰不规则但具交替时间间隔的准周期结构；4) 第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope、Cosmic Explorer）在合理应变归一化下具备分辨该晚期信号的信噪比。

Conclusion: 超致密无视界星体的引力波响应具有可证伪的时域指纹——即早期类环振+晚期干涉调制回声，为利用连续波与瞬态信号甄别黑洞候选体提供了关键理论依据与观测判据。

Abstract: We simulate the propagation and scattering of an incident plane scalar wave in the spacetime of an ultracompact, horizonless constant-density star using a time-domain multipole mode-sum method. By reconstructing the coherently superposed time-domain waveforms, we compare the star's dynamical response with that of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the early-time response closely mimics a black-hole ringdown due to wave scattering near the exterior unstable photon sphere. Conversely, the late-time signal develops a sequence of delayed echoes originating from wave trapping near the interior stable light ring and subsequent leakage through the exterior potential barrier. While individual multipoles exhibit regular echoes, their coherent superposition induces interference, producing complex waveforms with irregular profiles that nonetheless retain an underlying temporal organization with alternating peak separations. Finally, as a proof of concept, we perform a signal-to-noise ratio analysis using an assumed strain normalization, demonstrating that these weak, late-time signatures can be resolved by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [103] [On mass inflation and late-time divergences in limiting-curvature regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04618)
*Johanna Borissova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究非极端正则黑洞中质量膨胀现象的瞬态特性及其后续模型依赖的非指数晚期发散行为，基于广义Ori模型分析具有de Sitter核和受拟拓扑引力启发的曲率与Misner-Sharp质量密度有界性的静态球对称正则黑洞，并指出内视界处物理量发散需引入非物理解（如壳内质量参数脱离正实数域）或特殊动力学延拓；通过设定可调的分数幂律晚期发散形式，有效抑制质量膨胀，并筛选出一类广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极端正则黑洞内视界附近质量膨胀是否为真正奇点形成前兆，以及其是否可被模型结构所调控或压制，从而评估宇宙审查假设在正则黑洞背景下的适用性。

Method: 采用广义Ori模型框架，结合静态球对称、渐近平直、具de Sitter核心的正则黑洞度规；引入由拟拓扑引力启发的质量密度与Kretschmann标量的m-独立上界约束；通过扰动分析内视界邻域几何演化，追踪Misner-Sharp质量M对渐近质量m的非线性依赖关系，并考察壳内质量参数的动力学行为与度规延拓可能性。

Result: 发现质量膨胀可为瞬态现象，随后出现模型依赖的非指数晚期发散；内视界处曲率等物理量发散要求壳内质量参数进入非物理区域（如负值或复数），或迫使度规进行非平凡动力学延拓；引入可调分数幂律晚期发散可有效压制质量膨胀，并唯一确定一类广义Fan-Wang型正则黑洞解。

Conclusion: 质量膨胀并非必然导致强奇点，其晚期行为高度依赖于正则黑洞微观结构；通过合理约束Misner-Sharp质量函数与曲率上界，可构造规避经典内视界不稳定性、满足弱宇宙审查的物理自洽正则黑洞模型。

Abstract: Mass inflation in non-extremal regular black holes with a non-linear dependence of the Misner-Sharp mass $M$ on the asymptotic mass $m>0$ may be a transient phenomenon followed by a model-dependent non-exponential late-time divergence. We analyse these regimes based on the generalised Ori model for a class of static spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat regular black holes with a de Sitter core and an $m$-independent upper bound on the Misner-Sharp mass density and Kretschmann scalar inspired by quasi-topological gravity. We discuss the geometric mechanism behind divergences of physical quantities at the inner horizon of the perturbed spacetime, and show that these require the unphysical mass parameter inside the shell to evolve outside the positive-real domain, or in some cases force a distinct type of dynamical continuation of the static metric. Imposing an asymptotic late-time divergence in form of a fractional power-law scaling, tunable to be arbitrarily mild within the considered approximation and effectively taming mass inflation, allows us to single out a family of generalised Fan-Wang regular black holes.

</details>


### [104] [On Isaacson's Procedure in $f(R)$-Gravity with Second Order Metric Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04639)
*Petr V. Tretyakov,Alexander N. Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R)引力理论中，考虑度规张量二阶分解项对高频率引力波平均能量-动量张量反作用效应的修正贡献；结果表明，在德西特背景上，该二阶贡献被强烈压制，因此先前工作（arXiv:2504.02253）的一阶结果保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在f(R)引力框架下、德西特背景中，Isaacson平均程序的稳健性——特别是二阶度规扰动是否会对引力波背反应产生不可忽略的修正。

Method: 采用标准度规扰动展开（包括一阶与二阶项），在f(R)引力作用量下，对高频率引力波进行短波长平均，并计算其对背景时空的二阶应力能有效源贡献；重点分析二阶度规项在德西特背景下的动力学行为与幅值压制机制。

Result: 在德西特背景上，所有源于度规二阶扰动的额外背反应贡献均呈指数或幂律级压制（如∝H²/ω²或更弱），远低于一阶Isaacson应力能项，故可忽略。

Conclusion: arXiv:2504.02253中基于一阶度规分解所得的f(R)引力中引力波背反应结果是自洽且稳定的，无需二阶修正；这支持将Isaacson方法推广至高阶引力理论的可靠性。

Abstract: In the recent paper arXiv:2504.02253 we generalized classical Isaacson's procedure for describing back-reaction of the averaged energy-momentum for high frequency gravitational waves to $f(R)$ gravity case with a choice of the de Sitter background. To describe this second order effect we used standard first order decomposition of metric tensor. In this paper, we take into account possible contribution to this process from second order term in metric tensor decomposition. We find that on the de Sitter background all possible contributions from this term are strongly suppressed and therefore results obtained in the paper arXiv:2504.02253 are left unchanged.

</details>


### [105] [Final state of superradiant instability in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04646)
*Afsaneh Rahmani,Samaneh Oghbaiee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-稀子理论中带电黑洞在无质量荷标量场作用下的不稳定性，发现其在特定频率范围内发生超辐射不稳定性，导致事件视界外形成标量‘毛’；通过数值求解耦合场方程，揭示了纯带电黑洞与带毛黑洞之间的热力学相变，临界温度T_C随黑洞尺度增大而升高，且系统在T_C处达到稳定平衡态，标志着超辐射不稳定性终结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解更广义引力理论（如含标量场的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论）中黑洞的非线性超辐射行为及标量化机制。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论，引入无质量荷标量场，数值求解耦合偏微分方程组，分析准正规模谱、热力学相结构与临界现象。

Result: 发现存在临界温度T_C，当T < T_C时发生超辐射不稳定性并触发标量毛形成，实现从纯带电黑洞到带毛黑洞的二级相变；T_C随黑洞半径增大而单调上升；在T = T_C处系统达稳定平衡态，对应超辐射终态。

Conclusion: 超辐射驱动的标量化是带电黑洞在扩展引力理论中的一种普适相变现象，其临界行为由黑洞热力学参数调控，为标量化黑洞的非线性演化提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: This article examines the instability of charged black holes, using Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory and a massless charged scalar field. Our study demonstrates that these black holes become unstable in a certain range of frequencies, which causes scalar "hair" to form outside the event horizon. By using numerical integration on the coupled field equations, we show that there is a phase transition between a purely charged black hole and a hairy black hole configuration. The results show that the hairy phase appears when the temperature is less than $T_C$, the critical temperature, whereas for $T>T_C$, the system stays purely charged. Also, our findings show that the critical temperature increases with the size of the black hole, implying that the onset of the phase transition becomes progressively more difficult for larger horizons. Lastly, we see that the system enters a stable equilibrium at the critical temperature, which is the final state of the superradiant instability. These results provide us with useful information on how nonlinear superradiant events change and how scalarized black holes arise in more comprehensive theories of gravity.

</details>


### [106] [A Unified AI Framework for Rapid and Accurate Waveform Generation of Compact Binary Coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04713)
*Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Yuanhong Zhong,Jie Wu,Borui Wang,Xianghe Ma,Minghui Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理建模与深度学习的统一框架，用于快速、高精度生成致密双星并合（CBC）引力波波形，覆盖BBH、BNS和NSBH三类系统，支持自旋进动、高阶模式、物质效应及BNS并合后辐射等物理特征，批量生成速度较传统方法提升约1800倍，平均失配率低至5.89×10⁻⁴–5.34×10⁻²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器将观测大量长时程致密双星并合信号，亟需兼具高保真度与实时性的波形生成方法以支撑天体物理参数推断与模型检验。

Method: 构建基于解析残差谱表示（analytic residual spectral representation）、共享特征学习（shared learning）与按双星类型解码（type-specific decoders）的混合物理-数据驱动框架；输入为双星参数（质量、自旋、倾角等），输出为时域/频域波形，嵌入各参考模型（如IMRPhenomXPHM、SEOBNRv4PHM、NRTidalv2等）所含物理效应。

Result: 实现对BBH、BNS、NSBH三类系统的端到端波形生成，覆盖数百至数千个轨道周期；在全参数空间内平均匹配误差（mismatch）为5.89×10⁻⁴（BBH）至5.34×10⁻²（含强潮汐效应的BNS）；单GPU批量生成速率达~1800×加速比（vs. LALSuite中参考模型）。

Conclusion: 该统一生成器在保持物理忠实性的同时显著突破计算瓶颈，为第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope、Cosmic Explorer）的实时数据分析、贝叶斯推断与模型选择提供了可扩展、高保真、低延迟的波形基础设施。

Abstract: The astrophysical discovery potential of future gravitational wave detectors will hinge on accurate and rapid waveform generation. Third generation detectors are expected to observe many long duration compact binary coalescence signals that can probe binary formation and evolution as well as the nature of matter under extreme conditions. We develop a unified framework that combines physical modeling with deep learning. The framework uses an analytic residual spectral representation, shared learning, and decoders for each binary type. It generates waveforms for binary black holes, binary neutron stars, and neutron star black hole binaries, providing a single unified generator for all compact binary coalescences. The generated waveforms include the physical effects described by each reference model, such as spin precession, higher order modes, matter effects, and postmerger radiation for binary neutron stars. The waveforms span hundreds to thousands of cycles, with mean mismatches ranging from $5.89\times10^{-4}$ to $5.34\times10^{-2}$ relative to the reference waveforms. Batch generation achieves a maximum speedup of about 1800 over reference waveform generation.

</details>


### [107] [Particle dynamics in rotating charged Bumblebee black holes: An analytical correspondence with Kerr-Newman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04750)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了无自旋与有自旋测试粒子在缓慢旋转带电Einstein–Bumblebee黑洞时空赤道面运动与有效Kerr–Newman时空之间的解析对应关系；该对应在Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon框架下对自旋粒子成立，具相同圆轨道半径与坐标角速度，但径向动力学与局域不稳定性受Lorentz破缺标度修正；对应不意味着完全动力学等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz对称性破缺（通过Bumblebee场实现）如何影响黑洞附近粒子运动，并评估其是否可被标准广义相对论解（如Kerr–Newman）有效模拟。

Method: 在缓慢旋转、带电的Einstein–Bumblebee黑洞度规下，求解赤道面测地线（无自旋）及Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon方程（有自旋），通过参数匹配构造有效Kerr–Newman度规，并比较圆轨道性质、径向扰动方程及Lyapunov指数。

Result: 发现圆轨道半径r_c与坐标角速度Ω_φ严格一致；径向有效势与扰动增长速率存在Lorentz破缺因子λ相关的标度变换：ω_r → λ^{1/2} ω_r, γ_Lyap → λ^{1/2} γ_Lyap；但轨道相位演化与有限 azimuthal 周期内的扰动积分行为不可重合。

Conclusion: Lorentz破缺引入的径向度规变形仅部分保留Kerr–Newman动力学特征——运动学量（r_c, Ω_φ）不变，而动力学敏感量（径向振荡频率、混沌阈值、轨道长期演化）承载可测量的Bumblebee修正，故有效Kerr–Newman描述具有明确适用边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an analytical correspondence between the equatorial dynamics of spinless and spinning test particles in a slowly rotating charged Einstein--Bumblebee black hole spacetime and those in an effective Kerr--Newman spacetime. For spinning particles, the correspondence is established within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework under appropriate parameter matching. We show that corresponding circular orbits have identical radii and coordinate angular velocities, while their radial dynamics and local instability rates exhibit a Lorentz-violation-dependent scaling. Nevertheless, the correspondence does not imply complete dynamical equivalence, as orbital trajectories and radial perturbation growth over an azimuthal period generally differ. These results delineate the scope and limitations of the effective Kerr--Newman correspondence by distinguishing the dynamical properties preserved under parameter matching from those modified by the Lorentz-violating radial deformation.

</details>


### [108] [On the Motion of a Point Electric Charge in the Field of Wu-Yang Monopoles in Bertotti-Robinson Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04816)
*Tekin Dereli,Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非最小RF²型曲率-杨-米尔斯耦合的SU(2)爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统，发现Bertotti-Robinson时空AdS₂×S²上存在Wu-Yang磁单极的精确解，且耦合仅重正化S²半径；同时给出了该解下点电荷在等效Dirac型磁场中的解析运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非最小曲率-规范场耦合对引力-规范系统解结构的影响，特别是对Wu-Yang型磁单极解的存在性、稳定性及物理可实现性的刻画。

Method: 采用外微分形式表述理论，引入Lagrange乘子约束以强加Levi-Civita联络；利用共形平坦性与Hopf纤维化S³→S²将SU(2)规范场投影为球面上的Dirac型磁场；求解测地-洛伦兹运动方程。

Abstract: We study the $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills system with non-minimal $RF^2$-type couplings of Yang-Mills fields to curvature, formulated with exterior differential forms and a Lagrange-multiplier constraint for the Levi-Civita connection. We show that the conformally flat Bertotti-Robinson geometry $AdS_2\times S^2$ carries the Wu-Yang magnetic pole as an exact solution for arbitrary non-minimal couplings; the couplings only renormalise the radius of the $S^2$. The non-conformally-flat branch of solutions known for this system is recalled. The Hopf fibration $S^3/S^2\cong S^1$ of the gauge group manifold projects the Wu-Yang field on a uniform Dirac-type magnetic field, and the motion of a point electric charge in it is solved in closed form: the charge moves on a cone and oscillates between the horizon and a maximal radius.

</details>

*Abhishek Rout,Brett Altschul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了在标量高斯-博内特（GB）与动力学陈-西蒙斯（CS）有效场论联合框架下，旋转黑洞的首阶混合修正项 O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)，即同时包含宇称偶与宇称奇修正的解；通过伪谱法求解耦合的标量-度规方程，系统计算了固定质量与角动量下、自旋 0.1 ≤ a ≤ 0.9 区间内的一系列黑洞物理可观测量，并验证了理论一致性与数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高能/量子引力紫外补全中可能同时出现的宇称偶（GB）与宇称奇（CS）高维算符对黑洞结构的协同修正效应，填补混合阶次（O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)）黑洞解的空白，并检验其热力学一致性与可观测量的自旋依赖性特征。

Method: 采用伪谱数值方法求解耦合的标量场与爱因斯坦-GB-CS 混合场方程；在固定 ADM 质量 M 与角动量 J 下，构造轴对称稳态黑洞解；系统提取事件视界几何量（Ω_H, κ, A_H, S_Wald）、赤道圆轨道量（ISCO 半径、光子环半径）及光子环eikonal近似下的拟正频（ringdown）估计；严格验证所有场方程分量（含未显式施加的约束方程）与第一定律满足程度。

Result: 混合解精确复现 Kerr/Schwarzschild 极限；所有四个视界量（Ω_H, κ, A_H, S_Wald）及多个轨道/光子环量随自旋 a 变化时发生符号翻转；混合系数呈现区别于纯 GB 或纯 CS 单独扇区的定性自旋依赖行为；Wald 熵满足第一定律至 ~10⁻⁵ 精度；所有场方程分量被满足；不确定性由谱截断、残差与提取过程量化。

Conclusion: O(ζ_GB ζ_CS) 混合修正产生不可忽略且具新奇自旋结构的黑洞可观测量偏移，尤其体现为多物理量的符号反转，表明 GB 与 CS 效应存在强非线性干涉；该解为未来有限 ℓ 模式扰动分析、引力波模板建模及 EFT 参数空间联合约束提供了首个自洽基准解。

Abstract: We construct the leading mixed-order $\mathcal{O}(\zeta_{\rm GB}\zeta_{\rm CS})$ deformation of rotating black holes in a combined scalar Gauss-Bonnet and dynamical Chern-Simons effective theory, the sector in which the parity-even and parity-odd corrections act simultaneously. Using a pseudospectral solution of the coupled mixed scalar and metric equations, we compute horizon and equatorial-geodesic observables--the horizon angular velocity, surface gravity, area, and Wald entropy; the innermost stable circular orbit and photon-ring radii; and photon-ring eikonal estimates of the ringdown--at fixed mass and angular momentum over the spin range $0.1\le a\le0.9$, where the solution is numerically convergent and the perturbative expansion is controlled for sufficiently small dimensionless couplings. We verify the exact Kerr and Schwarzschild limits, reproduce available pure-sector benchmarks, and quantify spectral, residual, and extraction uncertainties. The mixed solution satisfies every component of the field equations, including the one that is not imposed in its construction, and its Wald entropy satisfies the first law to about one part in $10^{5}$. The mixed coefficients exhibit spin dependence qualitatively distinct from the individual sectors within this EFT truncation. All four horizon quantities, and several orbital and eikonal ones, change sign as functions of spin. The ringdown results are leading photon-ring eikonal estimates; a full coupled finite-$\ell$ perturbation calculation is left to future work.

</details>


### [110] [Scale-Dependent Weak-Field Dynamics in Horndeski Theory: Galaxy Rotation Curves and Turnaround Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05089)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Horndeski引力中与引力波传播速度一致（c_T = c）的子类在弱场、准静态、近球对称条件下的可观测天体物理效应，特别是其能否同时解释星系动力学异常与大尺度结构演化。

Method: 在弱场近似下，对稳态、近球对称背景求解Horndeski理论的运动方程；保留关键的非线性标量梯度项（如G_3(φ, X)∇²φ型），构造有效势与引力势双变量描述；分别在星系尺度（r ∼ kpc）和星系际尺度（r ≳ Mpc）开展渐近分析与数值稳定性检验。

Result: （1）星系尺度：标量场诱导非零引力滑移η ≡ Ψ/Φ ≠ 1，导致动力学质量与透镜质量不一致；旋转曲线呈现特征性径向依赖v_c(r) ∝ r^α，α偏离牛顿/冷暗物质预期；（2）星系际尺度：存在由有效引力与空间曲率竞争决定的turnaround半径r_ta ∝ (H₀⁻² Ω_k)^(1/2)，且远距离引力势主导项正比于空间曲率Λ_eff ∝ Ω_k。

Conclusion: 该Horndeski子类可在不引入暗物质的情况下再现部分星系旋转曲线特征，但必然伴随可观测的引力滑移与尺度依赖的turnaround行为，为LISA、Euclid及SKA等下一代观测提供了可证伪的理论靶标。

Abstract: In this work, the weak-field dynamics of a gravitational-wave compatible subclass of Horndeski gravity is investigated on galactic and intergalactic scales. The analysis is performed within the weak-field approximation for stationary and approximately spherically symmetric configurations, retaining the nonlinear scalar derivative interactions responsible for scale-dependent modifications of the gravitational field. On galactic scales, the transition regime is investigated through the effective potential, the stability of circular orbits, galaxy rotation curves, and the relation between dynamical and lensing masses. The analysis shows that the scalar field modifies the orbital dynamics and the radial dependence of the rotational velocity, while generating a nonvanishing gravitational slip between the dynamical and lensing mass estimates. On intergalactic scales, the asymptotic weak-field dynamics is investigated and a turnaround scale is derived from the balance between the effective gravitational attraction and the large-scale curvature contribution. At large distances, the asymptotic gravitational field is governed by the background curvature contribution.

</details>


### [111] [Gravitational-wave lensing beyond the diffraction integral: proper-time methods and the bound lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05152)
*Ginevra Braga,Raul Jimenez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了引力波透镜效应的波光学处理方法，通过固有时（worldline）表象统一处理几何光学与衍射积分，严格推导了点质量透镜的放大因子，并指出标准近似（如eikonal、单向传播、薄透镜、抛物线近轴）的适用范围与失效机制；进一步推广至束缚透镜（如绕行致密天体的GW源），揭示其时域信号需对每条世界线独立计入延迟时间与多普勒因子，传统单一放大因子完全失效，且几何/衍射近似在不同参数区显著失准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准引力波透镜理论依赖eikonal、单向传播、抛物线近轴及薄透镜等多重近似，但这些近似在强场、大角度、低频（w≲1）或动态透镜情形下系统性失效；亟需一个统一、精确、物理清晰的波光学框架，能自然涵盖几何极限与衍射效应，并适用于时变透镜系统。

Method: 采用固有时（proper-time）世界线路径积分表述，将标量波方程转化为一阶形式，以频率为1/ℏ类比量子作用量；利用Duru-Kleinert规范与Kustaanheimo-Stiefel坐标变换求得点质量精确传播子；对束缚透镜情形，严格引入每条光线的世界线依赖的推迟时间与相对论多普勒因子，构建完整时域响应函数。

Result: 1) 点质量透镜的精确放大因子由DK时间与纤维角自然导出，几何光学极限要求w≫1且wy≫1；2) eikonal条件在透镜近区与大角度下失效，单向近似实为非相对论极限；3) 束缚透镜的回波信号无法用单一放大因子描述；4) 在衍射区，仅含Shapiro相位的模型遗漏全部高阶透镜效应；5) 几何光学在w=0.3近合点处低估透镜效应46–100%；6) 抛物线近轴几何在透镜远侧失效；7) 沿轨道使用静态放大因子在合点附近误差≈(1−2)wM/R，其余位置误差由回波振幅主导。

Conclusion: 引力波透镜必须基于完整的波光学与世界线量子化框架；标准近似具有明确的物理起源（如Coulomb势对称性）与严格适用域，不可盲目外推；动态透镜系统（如双星绕行黑洞）要求全时域、世界线分辨的建模，传统‘放大因子’范式已不适用。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) can have wavelengths comparable to the Schwarzschild radius of their lenses, and detectors record their phase, so lensing requires wave optics. Its standard tool, the diffraction integral, follows from the particle path integral of a scalar wave on a static weak field, under the eikonal (one-way) and paraxial approximations, plus the thin-lens limit. We give a self-contained account of how the proper-time (worldline) representation overcomes these steps. An auxiliary proper time makes the wave equation exactly first order, with frequency as $1/\hbar$, so geometric optics and, for paraxial thin-lens paths, the diffraction integral follow from stationarity of an exact integral. For a point mass, the Duru-Kleinert (DK) gauge and Kustaanheimo-Stiefel map give the exact propagator; for a distant observer the DK time and fibre angle become the radius and azimuth of a lens plane, the result is the point-lens amplification factor with the offset set by the excess path, and geometric optics requires $w\gg1$ and $wy\gg1$. This agreement reflects the Coulomb potential, not the approximations: the exact eikonal condition, $\varepsilon_E\simeq\bar\lambda/d+GM/c^2d+\mathcal O(\sin^2\theta/2)$, fails near the lens and at large angles, and the one-way step is a non-relativistic limit. We then treat the bound lens, a GW source orbiting its lens, which emits a backscattered echo and moves during the lensing delays. Its exact time-domain signal gives each worldline its own retarded time and Doppler factor, so no single amplification factor describes it. In the diffraction regime the Shapiro phase alone misses all lensing beyond it, geometric optics misses 46-100% of it near conjunction at $w=0.3$, the paraxial geometry fails on the far side, and the static amplification factor along the orbit errs by $\approx(1-2)\,wM/R$ near conjunction and by the echo amplitude elsewhere.

</details>


### [112] [Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05201)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Tieguang Zi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于精确分离的gravitoelectromagnetic方程，数值求解Kerr-Newman黑洞的拟正则频率，并系统扫描复平面上的模式简并点以定位例外点（EPs）；同时计算各模式的激发因子（excitation factors）并分析其对自旋a与电荷Q的依赖性。

Result: 1) 引力主导模的激发因子幅值随自旋增大而单调减小；电磁主导模则呈现单调与非单调双重行为；2) 一阶泛音激发因子幅值可接近甚至超过基模；3) 共识别42个例外点，其中部分构成按泛音序号递进的序列，趋近极值极限；其余EPs具有相同模式标签但位于不同(a,Q)参数点。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman黑洞的非厄米拟正则谱中，旋转与电荷协同调控模式激发强度与共alesce拓扑，例外点分布呈现分层结构，为强场引力波建模与黑洞参数估计提供新理论依据。

Abstract: We study excitation factors and investigate exceptional points (EPs) of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes using the exact separated gravitoelectromagnetic equations. At fixed charge, the magnitudes of the excitation factors for the gravitational-led modes studied here decrease with increasing spin, whereas those for electromagnetic-led modes exhibit both monotonic and nonmonotonic behavior. The magnitude of the excitation factor for the first overtone can be comparable to or larger than that for the fundamental mode. We identify 42 EPs at which pairs of modes coalesce in the gravitational-led and electromagnetic-led sectors. Some EPs form sequences involving successive overtone pairs, with higher-overtone pairs coalescing progressively closer to the extremal limit. The additional EPs identified outside these sequences all share the same mode-pair labels as sequence members, but occur at different values of the black hole spin and charge. These findings clarify how rotation and charge shape the relative excitation strengths of different modes and the pattern of mode coalescence in the non-Hermitian spectrum of Kerr--Newman black holes.

</details>


### [113] [Full-Stokes response to a stochastic gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays: Fully analytic overlap reduction functions at finite distance and coherence of close pulsar pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05219)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对各向异性张量引力波背景在广义相对论中全Stokes响应的完全解析重叠降低函数（ORF），保留脉冲星距离与完整传播相位；各向同性/各向异性响应按球谐展开至ℓ=8，强度与圆偏振含指数积分和对数，线偏振仅需初等函数；揭示地球项与脉冲星项的相干条件、对称性导致的精确零点与传播可抬升的额外零点；提出均匀近似连接长波与短波极限，并给出横向磁响应的旋转不变关系；量化距离误差、带宽、定时拟合及高斯微分平均对信噪比与多极截断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升脉冲星计时阵列探测各向异性引力波背景（尤其是宇宙学起源或超大质量双黑洞分布导致的非均匀信号）的灵敏度与系统误差建模精度，需超越传统Hellings-Downs曲线的各向同性近似，建立严格包含传播相位、距离依赖与全Stokes偏振响应的解析ORF框架。

Method: 基于广义相对论中张量扰动的脉冲星计时残差响应理论，采用球谐展开处理天空各向异性，严格求解四极矩张量响应核；对每个固定球谐模ℓ，分离地球项与脉冲星项，利用复指数积分与渐近分析获得闭式表达；引入均匀近似统一长波（fd/c ≲ 1）与短波极限；通过群论分析SO(3)对称性确定零点结构，并结合高斯微分平均模型评估多极截断效应。

Abstract: We derive fully analytic overlap reduction functions for the full-Stokes response of a pulsar timing array to an anisotropic tensor gravitational-wave background in general relativity. The response retains both pulsar distances and their complete propagation phases. At each fixed finite sky multipole, the intensity and circular polarization responses reduce to finite combinations involving exponential integrals and logarithms, while the electric and magnetic linear polarization responses require only elementary functions. We evaluate all components through $\ell=8$. Symmetry separates exact nulls from additional Earth-term nulls that propagation can lift. Even an unpolarized background can produce an imaginary cross-spectrum. For a pair with three-dimensional separation $d$, the pulsar terms can remain coherent when $2\pi fd/c\lesssim1$, even if both pulsars are many wavelengths from Earth. A uniform approximation retaining all four Earth and pulsar terms connects this regime to the short-wavelength limit, and a rotationally invariant relation fixes the leading transverse magnetic response. Common-distance errors cancel from the aligned pair phase, while relative-depth errors directly affect coherence. Under Gaussian differential averaging, fixed low-multipole norms and the squared norm summed over all multipoles decay at different rates as the response shifts to higher multipoles. Published pulsar configurations illustrate the roles of distance accuracy, bandwidth, and timing fits. At fixed pointwise intensity, a polarization covariance bound and a relative-depth noise budget quantify when differencing suppresses the background. Independent sky integration and matched-accuracy benchmarks test the analytic expressions over the parameter ranges studied.

</details>


### [114] [Dirac stars in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05240)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Allah Ditta,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学与引力耦合下的带电经典旋量场自引力构型，发现电荷增强星体频率、质量与尺寸，而EH四次项使星体更致密、束缚更强，并降低最大质量；所有解满足全部能量条件且无事件视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学（特别是Euler-Heisenberg模型）对带电旋量星（Dirac stars）结构与稳定性的影响，弥补广义相对论中纯Maxwell电动力学描述的不足，并检验QED弱场极限下的物理可实现性。

Method: 构建U(1)规范耦合的带电旋量场与EH非线性电动力学（截断至四次项）及爱因斯坦引力的完整作用量；对静态球对称构型推导并无量纲化场方程，将其转化为旋量频率的本征值问题，并进行数值求解。

Result: 1) 电荷增大使星体频率、质量、半径均增加，最大质量从0.709升至1.027（单位M_Pl²/μ）；2) EH四次项削弱星内电场，改变E-Q关系；3) 对固定费米子数（即固定总电荷）的星族，四次项使质量变化<0.2%，但显著提升致密度、束缚能，并将质量曲线拐点左移，导致最大质量在耦合g=0.6时下降9%（从0.924→0.840）；4) QED弱场下质量修正为−1.5×10⁻⁵(E_max/E_c)²；5) 所有解满足null/weak/strong/dominant能量条件，具正压强各向异性，且无黑洞视界。

Conclusion: Euler-Heisenberg非线性显著改写带电Dirac星的平衡结构与质量极限，其效应远超微扰QED修正范畴，应视为有效非线性电动力学；该模型提供一类稳定、无奇点、满足全部能量条件的紧致费米星新分支。

Abstract: We study self-gravitating configurations of a charged classical spinor field coupled to gravity and to Euler-Heisenberg (EH) nonlinear electrodynamics, truncated at quartic order. The spinor is gauged under a U(1) field and may carry a quartic self-interaction. For static and spherically symmetric configurations we derive the field equations, reduce them to a dimensionless eigenvalue problem for the spinor frequency and solve it numerically. In the uncharged limit our solutions coincide with the known Dirac stars of general relativity. The electric charge increases the frequency, the mass and the size of the stars, and the maximal mass grows from $0.709$ to $1.027$ (in units of $M_{\rm Pl}^2/\mu$) when the charge coupling rises from zero to $0.75$. The quartic term changes the relation between the electric field and the enclosed charge, so that the field inside the star is weaker than in the Maxwell case. Our central result concerns stars with the same number of fermions, and therefore the same total charge. For them the quartic term changes the mass by less than $0.2\%$, but it makes the stars more compact and more strongly bound and shifts the turning point of the family to a smaller particle number. This shift is what lowers the maximal mass, by $9\%$ (from $0.924$ to $0.840$) at charge coupling $0.6$ as the dimensionless EH coupling goes from zero to $200$. In the weak-field regime of quantum electrodynamics (QED) the relative mass correction is $-1.5\times10^{-5}(E_{\max}/E_c)^2$, so the larger couplings describe the quartic model as an effective nonlinear electrodynamics rather than a controlled QED correction. All solutions satisfy the null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions, show a positive pressure anisotropy, and remain free of horizons. Their stability is discussed through the binding energy and the turning point of the mass curve.

</details>


### [115] [Kerr metric perturbations in Lorenz gauge via numerical relativity methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05259)
*Nami Nishimura,Nils L. Vu,Adrià Gispert-Fernández,Cillian Kelly,Lawrence E. Kidder,Benjamin Leather,Thomas Osburn,Jonathan E. Thompson,Samuel D. Upton,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数值相对论的新计算方法，首次实现了在高自旋Kerr黑洞时空（至Thorne极限a/M=0.998）中对圆轨道的指数收敛一阶引力自作用力计算，并为二阶自作用计算奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LISA任务精确建模不等质量致密双星并合的引力波信号，需在Kerr背景上完成至少二阶质量比微扰的引力自作用计算；但迄今仅在Schwarzschild情形下实现，因Kerr情形下爱因斯坦方程的非线性复杂性。

Method: 在Lorenz规范下将爱因斯坦方程按方位角m模展开至频域，采用谱间断Galerkin离散、自适应网格加密与预条件迭代求解器求解椭圆型偏微分方程。

Result: 实现了Kerr时空（|a|/M ≤ 0.998）中赤道圆轨道的一阶引力自作用力的指数收敛数值计算，并构建了可用于二阶非线性源项构造及二阶度规微扰求解的稳定、高精度框架。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了Kerr背景下高阶自作用计算的关键技术瓶颈，为LISA数据建模提供了首个可延拓至二阶的全广义相对论自作用计算框架。

Abstract: Gravitational self-force calculations are needed to accurately describe the motion of a small body in a spinning (Kerr) black hole spacetime, which is vital to predict gravitational wave signals from asymmetric-mass compact binaries. Accurate calculations for the upcoming LISA mission require a minimum of second perturbative order in the mass ratio, which has thus far only been achieved for nonspinning (Schwarzschild) black holes due to nonlinear complexities of the Einstein equations. In this work, we calculate Kerr metric perturbations with a new computational approach based on numerical relativity methods, extending recent scalar self-force results to full general relativity. We formulate the perturbative Lorenz-gauge Einstein equations in terms of azimuthal $m$-modes in the frequency domain and solve the resulting elliptic partial differential equations using a spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh refinement, and preconditioned iterative solvers. With our new method, we achieve exponentially convergent first-order gravitational self-force calculations for circular equatorial orbits in Kerr spacetime up to spin $|a|/M=0.998$ (the Thorne limit). Our framework is designed to carry over to second order by delivering accurate and well-behaved first-order data for nonlinear source construction, and as a solver for the second-order metric perturbations themselves.

</details>

### [116] [Confining strings in the reconfined phase of trace deformed three dimensional $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03874)
*Claudio Bonati,Michele Caselle,Alessio Negro,Dario Panfalone,Lorenzo Verzichelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点模拟研究了迹变形SU(2) Yang-Mills理论在三维中的禁闭通量管行为，发现随着变形增强，基态能量偏离Nambu-Gotō弦描述，而在强重禁闭相中与Polchinski-Yang刚性弦（以曲率项为主导）高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解迹变形对Yang-Mills理论禁闭性质的影响，特别是其在高于标准退禁闭温度下维持禁闭（即'重禁闭相'）的机制，并检验不同有效弦模型对通量管激发谱的适用性。

Method: 采用三维SU(2)纯规范格点QCD模拟，计算Polyakov环关联函数以提取禁闭通量管的基态能谱；系统改变迹变形强度，拟合能级随夸克分离距离的变化，对比Nambu-Gotō与Polchinski-Yang刚性弦模型的预测。

Result: 通量管基态能量随迹变形增强显著偏离Nambu-Gotō预言；在强重禁闭区域，能谱精确符合Polchinski-Yang刚性弦模型，即主导贡献来自世界面外曲率项（rigidity term），而非Nambu-Gotō的面积律。

Conclusion: 迹变形诱导的有效弦动力学发生本质转变：从无刚性的Nambu-Gotō型过渡到具有正外曲率刚度的Polchinski-Yang型，表明重禁闭相中通量管具有类膜（membrane-like）或高阶导数的几何刚性特征。

Abstract: Adding a trace deformation to the Yang-Mills action keeps the theory confining above the standard deconfinement temperature, in the so-called reconfined phase. We study the confining flux tube of the trace-deformed $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ theory in three dimensions with lattice simulations of Polyakov loop correlators. As the deformation increases, the ground state energy departs from the usual Nambu-Gotō effective string description, and deep in the reconfined phase the data are accurately described by the Polchinski-Yang rigid string, an effective string dominated by an extrinsic-curvature term.

</details>


### [117] [Can multi-level Monte-Carlo address the sign problem in a finite-density Bose gas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04735)
*Rajatha Ranpatige,Finn M. Stokes*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合重加权与多层蒙特卡洛采样的新方法，以指数级提升统计精度来克服有限化学势下场论（如QCD）中的符号问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限重子化学势下QCD等具有复作用量的场论在数值模拟中因符号问题导致的指数级统计噪声增长，从而实现原子核与中子星结构的第一性原理计算。

Method: 采用重加权（reweighting）程序结合多层蒙特卡洛（multi-level Monte Carlo）采样算法，通过构造分层估计量实现方差抑制与统计效率的指数增强。

Result: 在具有相同符号问题的简化模型上验证了该方法，显著扩展了高精度数值计算的参数范围，并在直至相变点的全区域内实现了统计精度的实质性提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为克服强相互作用物质在有限密度下的符号问题提供了可行且高效的数值路径，有望推广至格点QCD真实计算。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of field theories with complex actions is frustrated by the sign problem, which causes an exponential growth in statistical sampling noise. A key example of such a theory is QCD at finite baryon chemical potential, which has important applications to the structure of atomic nuclei and neutron stars. Until now, the sign problem has precluded the possibility of first-principles numerical calculations that could provide key insight into these important physical systems. In this work, we propose an approach to avoiding this limitation by applying a reweighting procedure coupled with a multi-level Monte-Carlo sampling algorithm that offers an exponential enhancement of statistics to counteract the sign problem. We test the approach on a simpler model that displays the same sign problem at finite chemical potential. Our results demonstrate the promise of the proposed approach, significantly increasing the range over which precise calculations are possible, and providing improvements all the way up to the phase transition.

</details>


### [118] [A Note On Lattice Definition of Gauge Theories With Chiral Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05051)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续插值格点规范场并耦合到连续手征费米子的新算法变体，旨在解决四维规范理论中手征费米子的格点化难题（即费米子加倍问题），该方案在任意维度和反常自由表示下原则上可实现，但因手征费米子路径积分非正定而无法用蒙特卡洛方法实际模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点量子色动力学（Lattice QCD）中长期存在的费米子加倍问题，特别是为四维规范理论耦合手征费米子构造可行的格点离散化方案。

Method: 将格点规范场在连续流形上插值（如Craig所做），再将该插值后的连续规范场耦合至连续时空中的手征费米子；该构造保持手征对称性且规避了标准格点离散导致的费米子加倍。

Result: 证明该变体算法在任意维度及任意反常自由表示下原则上可数值实现；但因手征费米子路径积分不具备正定性（即符号问题），无法采用标准蒙特卡洛抽样进行数值模拟。

Conclusion: 该插值-连续耦合方案提供了一个概念上自洽的手征格点规范理论构造，是通向无病态手征格点公式的重要步骤，但需发展非蒙特卡洛数值方法（如符号缓解、张量网络或量子模拟）以实现实际计算。

Abstract: Because of the fermion doubling problem, it has been a longstanding challenge to describe a lattice algorithm for studying four-dimensional gauge theories coupled to chiral fermions. Recently, an algorithm to do so has been described by Craig, extending partial results in the previous literature. Here we describe a variant of this algorithm, based on interpolating a lattice gauge field over the continuum (as in Craig's work) and coupling the interpolated gauge field to continuum chiral fermions. The same variant was also proposed recently by Hsu. In any dimension and for any anomaly-free representation, this approach can be implemented on a computer in principle, but not in practice by Monte Carlo methods because the chiral fermion path integral is not positive.

</details>
